# Birth by Sleep Final Mix research audit

Audit date: 2026-10-01. Repository baseline: `f933ab1` on `research/audit-2026-10-01`. This is an audit of repository evidence, not new external research or certification. The original audit changed only this report; subsequent status updates are dated separately below.

Scope: Steam HD Final Mix; Terra, Ventus, Aqua main stories; Aqua’s **Final Episode** and **Secret Episode** as separate save/acquisition contexts. The BBS Secret Episode is included; the standalone **KH0.2** is excluded. Shared enemy/command definitions never imply shared character progress. “Unknown acquisition” does not mean unobtainable. No user playthrough is a prerequisite: `ai_docs/testing-and-content-validation.md:5–9` requires sourced edition-correct facts and app validation separately.

## Current resolution status

All 38 findings now have researched dispositions: **17 partial**, **4 researched-open**, **12 closed**, **2 provenance**, **1 implementation**, **1 visual**, **1 excluded**. See the [live resolution log](research-resolution-2026-10-01.md) and [complete per-ID ledger](research-dispositions-2026-10-01.json). These supersede baseline statuses, counts and claims below. Closed scopes are BBS-005/007/008/009/014/017/020/023/024/038; partial catalogs are not described as complete.

## Method and measured coverage (baseline)

Read the full BBS research pack, specification/readiness, both September 28 research supplements, implementation/review/reference notes, source manifest/Drive audit, legacy melding input, current generators, generated JSON, runtime model/journal/solver and tests. Parsed every JSON/CSV record, checked absent fields and null/empty/zero semantics, compared canonical and generated records, audited recipe quarantines and source-name joins, and searched tracked text broadly for uncertainty, missing-data, TODO, candidate, conflict, source/edition and acceptance language. Source lines below refer to this baseline, not this report. JSON paths use zero-based array indices.

The appendices enumerate affected record IDs and source occurrences rather than treating repeated generated text as independent discoveries. Generated JSON is retained as an occurrence, with a canonical mapping, rather than counted again as a research issue. No generator was run to modify application data; no tests or external sources were executed for this documentation-only pass. Existing test claims are identified as historical evidence.

| Inventory | Measured count / boundary |
|---|---|
| Current guide | 1368 entries; 483 recipes (459 melding, 24 ice cream); 489 nonempty uncertainty fields |
| Current entry categories | treasures: 383; stickers: 60; keyblades: 48; abilities: 90; finishers: 46; materials: 286; commands: 246; reports: 10; challenges: 27; album: 15; arena: 48; minigames: 57; episodes: 2; achievements: 34; bestiary: 16 |
| Collectible CSV | 443 records: 374 numbered main chests (T122/V130/A122), 8 Secret Episode chests, 1 tutorial acquisition, 60 stickers; 442 world-counted records |
| Source melding | 296 outcome rows; 112 standard crystal/type mappings; 465 original character/input/level groups, including 3 malformed 200% groups; 5 outcome rows excluded together across 2 input pairs |
| UI model | 139 known command identities; 459 scoped input groups; 46 finisher nodes (T15/V15/A16); 108 shops; 9 crystals; 30 ability stack rows |
| Acquisition reference | 14 distinct ice cream recipes; 42 flavor leads; 42 finish predicate rows; 108 command-shop candidates, of which 38 have a dash shop level |
| Crystal routes | 11 crystal/enemy records, 9 unique enemies, all without exact area or character restrictions; 9 crystal types covered only by examples |
| Equipment / abilities | 24 weapon forms → 48 scoped entries (T16/V15/A15 + 2 Aqua episode weapons); 30 abilities → 90 scoped entries |
| Sources | 70 inspected community source objects; 3 primary-source objects; workbook 3 full historical grid reads; later September 28 metadata-only recheck |

## Findings register

**OPEN** means a factual question or explicit coverage gap remains. **ANSWERED / NOT INTEGRATED** means a repository answer exists but some checked-in views still omit it. **PARTLY ANSWERED** separates a recovered fact from a remaining uncertainty. **PROVENANCE**, **IMPLEMENTATION**, **VISUAL**, and **HISTORICAL / EXCLUDED** are not additional unknown gameplay facts. The stable BBS IDs below are deduplicated; Appendix tables preserve every repeated affected data record.

### BBS-001 — Precise collectible directions

**Current disposition (2026-10-01): partial.** Systematically reviewed all 382 guide chest rows: 374 main chests now have linked source approach/landmark text, plus the independently sourced Secret Gem route and all 60 sticker pickups. Reconciled three mismatches: Terra Tower Entrance, Ventus Rainbow Falls: Base, and Aqua chest4 Mega Magic Recipe (saved IDs retained). See [complete per-ID evidence and residual ledger](research-dispositions-2026-10-01.json).

**Historical baseline finding:**

**Status: OPEN.** All 443 CSV records carry “area-only; precise directions pending”; exact pickup position, approach/action and repeated-chest disambiguation are absent as a complete inventory. Runtime adds three limited directions: Terra Fission Firaga, Ventus Mine Entrance Balloon Sticker, Aqua Deep Space Dale Sticker. Thus 440 still have only the generic “Chest/Sticker pickup in …” direction; those three are partial exceptions, not fully validated routes. The runtime retains the blanket uncertainty even for them. Appendix A enumerates all 443 IDs, canonical CSV line and runtime path; sources are the row’s world/album URLs.

Evidence: `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/README.md:18`; `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/collectibles-and-reports.md:68`–74; `src/games/bbsfm/generate.py:17`–22; `ai_docs/readiness/birth-by-sleep-final-mix.md:29`–31.

### BBS-002 — Official Reports order and inventory completeness

**Current disposition (2026-10-01): researched-open.** Preserved 374 main, eight Secret and one tutorial chest records; fixed generated collectible flags so all eight Secret chests count (442 total including 60 stickers). See [complete per-ID evidence and residual ledger](research-dispositions-2026-10-01.json).

**Historical baseline finding:**

**Status: OPEN.** Independently reconcile main T122/V130/A122 chest totals and their official Reports sequence; verify the separate eight Secret chests and 20 stickers per character. `source_position` is source numbering, not certified Reports order. Runtime `order=i` is extraction order. Ventus tutorial Sliding Dash intentionally has blank source position and is outside Reports/world totals; that blank is not missing official numbering. Sticker IDs deliberately use name+area. Appendix A covers all records; the counted denominator is 442, not 443.

### BBS-003 — Access timing, movement requirements and returnability

**Current disposition (2026-10-01): partial.** Integrated post-Mad-Treant access for Ventus Soothing Crystal and source-described movement prerequisites in sticker pickup notes. See [complete per-ID evidence and residual ledger](research-dispositions-2026-10-01.json).

**Historical baseline finding:**

**Status: OPEN.** For all collectibles establish earliest access, story/episode revisit restrictions, exact movement minimum levels and alternate approaches. In particular, do Terra/Aqua’s four Land of Departure chests remain reachable through late revisit/Unknown access? Do not call them permanently missable yet. Ventus Dwarf Woodlands Soothing Crystal #4 has known post-Mad-Treant timing, but this timing is not carried into generated instructions. Aqua’s Dale Sticker requires High Jump/Air Slide, with minimum levels explicitly unverified. Separate original main-save access from Aqua Final Episode’s unavailable Land of Departure.

Evidence: `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/collectibles-and-reports.md:22`, `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/collectibles-and-reports.md:69`–74; `src/games/bbsfm/generate.py:22`; `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/challenges-and-unlocks.md:76`. Appendix A lists IDs.

### BBS-009 — Complete modern command identity, type and character eligibility catalog

**Current disposition (2026-10-01): closed.** Canonical persistent HD catalog now has 187 identities with independently table-backed types and character eligibility: 40 Attack, 57 Magic, 23 Item, 15 Friendship, 17 Movement, 10 Defense, 8 Reprisal, 17 Shotlock. Exclusion records distinguish temporary, multiplayer and HD-removed commands. See [complete per-ID evidence and residual ledger](research-dispositions-2026-10-01.json).

**Historical baseline finding:**

**Status: OPEN.** 139 UI identities are a union of shop, surviving meld and typed chest names, not a verified command roster. Character badges infer eligibility from those occurrences; presence as a recipe ingredient must not substitute for independently established ordinary acquisition/usability. Missing route != unavailable. Taxonomy counts: Attack 40, Magic 57, Item 8, Movement 13, Defense 10, Reprisal 7, Shotlock 3, Other 1, Friendship 0. “Balloon Letter” is Other. The legacy 150-row list includes 17 Friendship commands, lacks Item/Shotlock groups, and may contain HD-removed/temporary commands; verify inclusion rather than treating all absent legacy names as missing obtainable commands. Appendix E lists all 139 known IDs/types/character flags and every legacy-only name.

Evidence: `ai_docs/research/bbsfm-2026-09-28-coverage-and-gap-research.md:124`–128; `ai_docs/research/bbsfm-2026-09-28-coverage-and-gap-research.md:156`; `ai_docs/research/bbsfm-category-scope-matrix-2026-09-28.md:23`; `src/games/bbsfm/build-ui-data.py:25–38`; `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/acquisition-tables.json:2227`; `src/journal/BbsMelding.tsx:24–25`. Existing lead: `Deck_Command_(KHBBS)`.

### BBS-020 — Flavor/Prize Pod exact farming routes

**Current disposition (2026-10-01): closed.** All 42 flavor records now join character-scoped room/event routes across 22 Prize Pod route records. Spawn-wave/interaction notes and world reset guidance are integrated; Arena replay reset is kept separate. See [complete per-ID evidence and residual ledger](research-dispositions-2026-10-01.json).

**Historical baseline finding:**

**Status: OPEN.** 42 flavors only have character and world/event leads, yielding scoped material records with unnormalized spawn/reset notes. Normalize locations, encounter triggers, unwanted-spawn resets, rooms, character access and Arena/event alternatives. ### Coverage and runtime fallback statements

`src/games/bbsfm/content.json:27076` (`$.coverage`) repeats **“Exact pickup directions and farming routes are partial”**, **“Melding excludes both conflicted input pairs”**, **“hidden goals and exact save aggregation remain incomplete”**, and **“full challenge/command/enemy catalogs are not yet certified.”** Canonical authoring occurrence: `src/games/bbsfm/generate.py:156`; covered by BBS-001/006/007/009/015/025/029/032. Runtime fallback occurrences **“No Moogle purchase documented”**, **“No enemy drop route documented here”**, **“No chest route documented”**, **“No ... recipe documented”**, and **“No documented recipe at these command levels”** all occur in `src/journal/BbsMelding.tsx:47`; these are unknown/absent-data states whose affected catalog identities are enumerated in Appendix E. All 71 generated flavor materials, 27 crystal materials and 6 acquisition-unspecified command materials have their exact caveat fields listed in Appendix B.

## Appendix C — quarantined and normalized recipe occurrences

The canonical source is `melding-reference.json`; both generators exclude complete unordered name+level input pairs. `content.json` recipes and `ui-data.json` groups are generated views of those pairs and are not separate factual evidence. Three original malformed groups are not three different source defects.

## Appendix E — command catalog coverage and legacy exclusions

BBS-009/010/011/012 apply to all known catalog identities. These are evidence-derived flags, not a certified availability matrix. Each UI `$.commands[i]` is generated by `src/games/bbsfm/build-ui-data.py:25–38`; names are identities because this array lacks explicit IDs. Stable collection/material IDs where present use `bbsfm:<character>:command|material:<slug>`. Catalog-only results do not automatically have a command checklist record. “No purchase/drop/chest/producing recipe documented” in `src/journal/BbsMelding.tsx:47` means unknown/absent from this guide, never impossible. The table explicitly counts ordinary-command records and source-route evidence; no absent route is converted to unavailability.

## Appendix H — source-manifest qualifier occurrences

All community records retain “community reference inspected; not in-game verified” and “Structured tables extracted; task-relevant facts reviewed. No claim of line-by-line narrative review.” These are historical inspection limits (BBS-033), not automatic missing facts. The exact pages remain source leads for the corresponding issue. Manifest primary_sources[1] public hidden-description limit belongs to BBS-032; legacy_workbook.reads[1].populated_location_cells=0 belongs to BBS-015/034. Reused-original SHA/Library ID is provenance, not a new source inspection.

## Appendix I — structural omissions versus intentional blanks

Missing fields are meaningful only against the required data contract; absent optional fields do not prove a game fact is unknown. This inventory distinguishes the actual unresearched schema dimensions from deliberate nulls. Where a whole array lacks a field, its full record list above is the occurrence enumeration.

| Dataset / exact defining location | Missing or empty dimension | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/collectible-inventory.csv:1` | No precise-directions/access/movement/returnability/placement/official-order evidence columns; source_position blank for tutorial/stickers | OPEN001–004; every row in Appendix A; intentional unnumbered status is not itself open |
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/acquisition-tables.json:1224` | Every108row lacks route ID, currency discriminator, medal price, alternate initial/first-acquisition source and Boolean predicates | OPEN009/010; Appendix D enumerates |
| `src/games/bbsfm/ui-data.json:3` | Every139row only name/type/characters; no CP curves/max levels/effects/route evidence IDs | OPEN009/012; Appendix E enumerates |
| `src/games/bbsfm/crystal-sources.json:6` | All11source objects: world-only locations; zero area or characters fields | OPEN015; all11 enumerated in B |
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/keyblades.csv:1` | 24forms have no reach/passive columns | OPEN018; Appendix F |
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/acquisition-tables.json:381` | 42world_or_event strings; no normalized room/spawn/reset or route alternatives | OPEN020; Appendix D |
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/acquisition-tables.json:752` | 3parent_equipped nulls are initial Finish; non-style nulls and initial target0 are deliberate | HISTORICAL/EXCLUDED037; every null/zero below |
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/melding-reference.json:194` | Zero character success rates / missing character keys mean ineligible outcome, not unknown probability | HISTORICAL/EXCLUDED037; preserve source positive-rate filtering |
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/ability-stacks.csv:22`; `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/ability-stacks.csv:30` | Zero EXP / Scan N/A crystal and dash recipe_types | Known non-meld condition; actual acquisition/effect detail OPEN014 |
| `src/games/bbsfm/generate.py:101` | Empty Arena rewards initialized then selectively populated | Not proof no reward; OPEN021/022; all48actual records in F |
| src/journal/BbsMelding.tsx:47 | No route documented / no recipe documented fallback labels | Unknown or not represented; do not equate to impossible; OPEN009–011 |
| `ai_docs/games/bbsfm/melding-reference.json:8671` | Candidate audit, conflict_rows_zero_based[90,182] | Known narrow defects006/007, not blanket rejection of296rows |

## Appendix J — duplicate exclusions, scan scope and reproducibility

Canonical mapping, not independent evidence:

- `collectible-inventory.csv` → `src/games/bbsfm/generate.py:14–22` → all 443 matching `content.json.entries` IDs (Appendix A).
- `melding-reference.json.recipes` → unordered character/input/level groups in `src/games/bbsfm/generate.py:46–72` and `src/games/bbsfm/build-ui-data.py:11–24` →459`content.json.recipes` and 459`ui-data.json.groups`. Both exclude the same five raw rows/two pairs (Appendix C). All remaining groups sum 100%; this does not certify names/catalog completeness.
- `acquisition-tables.json.command_shop.rows` →108`ui-data.shops` and 246`content.entries` commands (Appendix D).
- `acquisition-tables.json.flavors.rows` →character-scoped ingredient/material rows; raw 42 rows and generated uncertainty rows are in D/B.
- `acquisition-tables.json.ice_cream.recipes` →24 scoped recipes; `finish_unlocks.rows` →46 entries+46 UI nodes (Appendix F).
- `keyblades.csv` →48 entries; `ability-stacks.csv` →90 entries+30 UI stack copies (Appendix F).
- `materials-and-equipment.md` drop prose →16 bestiary entries (`src/games/bbsfm/generate.py:145–154`); `crystal-sources.json` is a newer, separate 11 row researched world-source overlay, not a complete replica. Differences are audited under 015/017.
- Prototype `preview/` contains generated/bundled duplicates of prototype shell/style/scene and production stand-in imagery. They provide no additional gameplay evidence and are not counted again. Image binaries were inventoried as visual evidence; this pass does not reinterpret their pixels as new game facts.

Scanned tracked-text inventory follows. All BBS-specific text files listed were parsed/read for this audit. Shared files were searched for BBS-context sections and global contracts; unrelated other-game facts are excluded. Binary image/model assets, generated bundles/package lockfiles and mirrored prototype preview payloads were inventoried, not treated as factual prose. Full source files are retained in the repository; external URLs here are existing research leads and were not revisited.

Validation of this report: all referenced repository paths exist; JSON paths/line positions in generated appendices were calculated from parsed source offsets; CSV IDs resolve to current guide entries; all 443 collectible records and all 489 nonempty uncertainty fields are accounted for. Raw null/empty status was reviewed by meaning. No source files, generated data, user progress, external documents or gameplay state were changed. Completeness means all repository-discoverable BBS gaps and caveats were inventoried; it does not assert that unknown facts absent from every repository source have been magically discovered.
